<div class="body with-sidebar body--default">
  <div class="body__main stack ">
          <h2 class="body__heading">
            <div>Self-Help Resources</div>
      </h2>
    
          <div class="body__content word-wrap">
        
                  
            <div><p>For those seeking to understand how the appellate process works, view the short video or connect with additional resources on the <a href="https://selfhelp.appellate.courts.ca.gov/">Courts of Appeal Self-Help website</a>.  The site may help anyone who is involved in an appeal, especially those without a lawyer who are representing themselves. </p>
<p><a class="button button--secondary jcc-button--secondary" data-link-type="link" href="https://selfhelp.appellate.courts.ca.gov/">Get Self Help</a></p>
</div>

<div class="body with-sidebar body--default">
  <div class="body__main stack ">
          <h2 class="body__heading">
            <div>The California Appellate Court Legacy Project</div>
      </h2>
    
          <div class="body__content word-wrap">
                  <div class="body__lead">
            <div><p>The Legacy Project provides a historical record of the personal experiences of individual justices and the changes that have emerged over the years in the California judicial system and the legal profession.</p>
</div>
      </div>
        
                  
            <div><p><div data-embed-button="paragraphs_inline_entity_form" data-entity-embed-display="view_mode:paragraph.preview" data-entity-type="paragraph" data-entity-uuid="53b2baa1-1f10-4f0a-bedf-ff57611436c4" data-langcode="en" data-entity-embed-display-settings="[]" class="embedded-entity">




  <a href="/about-courts/california-appellate-court-legacy-project" class="button button--primary button--normal icon--arrow" hreflang="en">Read More on the Legacy Project</a>
</div>
</p>
